```
# Pebrel 1.8.1  ## English  ### Added  - Added seven scrollback limits: 1,000 / 2,000 / 5,000 / 10,000 / 20,000 / 50,000 / 100,000 lines. The default remains 10,000; changes apply to new terminals without truncating open sessions. - Added a wheel-speed control from 0.25× to 4.00×, defaulting to 1.00×. Dragging previews the speed and releasing saves it; trackpad pixel scrolling, font zoom and completion-list scrolling keep their existing behavior. - Added a custom theme library with saved snapshots, a preview editor, and theme import/export. Saved themes can retain terminal colors, typography and window appearance alongside the built-in themes. - Added optional background update downloads and an option to install a verified download on the next application launch on Windows.  ### Fixed  - Fixed command history and completion candidates leaking between the parent shell and typed SSH/WSL connections. Integrated shell reports switch the active context and restore it on return; native PowerShell predictions retain the user's configuration. - Restored the default terminal font to the exact Maple Mono Normal NF CN font bundled with 1.7.0 after the default changed in 1.8.0. Explicitly selected custom fonts remain available. Addresses [#148](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/148). - Fixed update checks and installer downloads ignoring the application's proxy configuration, including proxy exclusions and SOCKS connections. - Fixed scheduled updates merging saved windows into one window. Update recovery retains each window's tabs and active tab; this does not change desktop position or window-size restoration. - Fixed the Windows installer failing to reuse its registered installation directory when updating an existing installation. - Restored the 1.7 tab-status alignment and prevented status glyphs from being clipped.  ### Improved  - Reduced unused scrollback allocations and reclaimed oversized row capacity when narrowing terminals, while preserving retained history and content. Large width reductions can take longer during synchronous reclamation; savings depend on the workload. - Improved Pi conversation recovery by retaining the native session ID and session file together with each pane's shell and working directory. Failed restoration keeps the original target available for retry instead of treating command submission as successful recovery. Addresses [#146](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/146). - Improved Windows update handoff: save workspace state before exit, wait for the old process to stop before installing, verify the installer, and retain failure details when installation or restart does not complete. - Improved cleanup of terminals on Windows when panes close, including console resources and process-monitoring handles; retained font ownership while text rendering still needs it.  Windows packages include a Pebrel installer and portable ZIP. Linux x64 packages and macOS Apple Silicon/Intel DMGs retain their Preview designation. macOS requires version 14 or later. Molecular structure rendering remains disabled.  ## 中文  ### 新增  - 新增：七档回滚行数：1,000 / 2,000 / 5,000 / 10,000 / 20,000 / 50,000 / 100,000。默认仍为 10,000，仅影响新建终端，不截断已打开会话的历史。 - 新增：0.25×～4.00× 滚轮速度控制，默认 1.00×；拖动即时预览，松手保存。触控板像素滚动、字体缩放和补全列表滚动保持原有行为。 - 新增：自定义主题库，支持保存主题快照、预览编辑和主题导入导出；可在内置主题之外保存终端配色、字体排版及窗口外观。 - 新增：Windows 可选的后台更新下载，以及在下次启动应用时安装已校验下载包的选项。  ### 修复  - 修复：父 Shell 与手动进入的 SSH／WSL 连接之间混用命令历史和补全候选的问题；根据集成 Shell 的回执切换当前环境，返回后恢复父环境，并保留用户配置的 PowerShell 原生预测。 - 修复：将 1.8.0 更换后的默认终端字体恢复为与 1.7.0 完全相同的内置 Maple Mono Normal NF CN；用户明确选择的自定义字体仍可使用。对应 [#148](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/148)。 - 修复：检查更新和下载安装器时忽略应用代理配置的问题，包括代理排除规则与 SOCKS 连接。 - 修复：计划更新将已保存的多个窗口合并成一个窗口的问题；更新恢复时保留各窗口的标签及活动标签，不改变桌面位置或窗口尺寸的恢复行为。 - 修复：Windows 安装器更新已有安装时未复用已注册安装目录的问题。 - 修复：恢复 1.7 的标签状态对齐方式，避免状态符号被裁切。  ### 改进  - 改进：减少闲置回滚行的预分配，并在缩窄终端时回收过大的行容量，保留历史行数及内容；大幅缩列时同步回收可能增加耗时，节省量取决于实际负载。 - 改进：Pi 会话恢复同时保留原生会话 ID、会话文件，以及各窗格的 Shell 和工作目录。恢复失败时保留原目标以便重试，不再仅凭恢复命令已提交就视为恢复成功。对应 [#146](https://github.com/Kuddev/nebula/issues/146)。 - 改进：Windows 更新交接在退出前保存工作区，等待旧进程退出后再安装，校验安装包，并在安装或重启未完成时保留失败详情。 - 改进：Windows 关闭窗格时释放控制台资源及进程监测句柄，并在文字渲染仍需使用字体时保持其有效。  Windows 提供 Pebrel 安装器和 ZIP 便携包；Linux x64 包以及 macOS Apple Silicon／Intel DMG 继续标记为 Preview。macOS 最低运行版本为 14。分子结构渲染仍处于禁用状态。  ---  **SHA256**  - `Pebrel-v1.8.1-windows-x64.zip`: `4e6315d47c4b8d1db612a7ada0231c88b570dfd7dd1faec56bd498a8a2face18` - `Pebrel-v1.8.1-windows-x64-setup.exe`: `1b94feebd50d135fa6e7330830659dc38457b4f5b4f6216eeabd64a1e415eccf` - `Pebrel-v1.8.1-linux-x64-preview.AppImage`: `7b098a1806e20c22c6d34a848be26207108e9ceae9d15522b8ae0b2319c15aca` - `Pebrel-v1.8.1-linux-x64-preview.deb`: `0b218ceda32e96f92eadec33e166211222b71faf4661cf54a4b8824789f7a18d` - `Pebrel-v1.8.1-linux-x64-preview.tar.gz`: `26b5e01f543a014cf6250fc7dbfc8db9d19c38e5549f0c433b03637dc6550ea0` - `Pebrel-v1.8.1-macos-arm64-preview.dmg`: `e1f0858bb507185fd4903ce2ca8308cf88d7e07172357c3f33b1f246a3d35c7f` - `Pebrel-v1.8.1-macos-x64-preview.dmg`: `5b2dcd220a958646b04c8a6425f314ed58505ec4a015d141454cf23f75617de4`
```

```
# 中文  本次更新将 DeepSeek Harness 升级至 `v0.1.5-rc.2`，并调整各平台打包方式，完善打包后的运行时与内置技能检查。稳定版与测试版包含相同的运行时升级和打包改进。  DSH Desktop 是社区维护的开源项目，并非 DeepSeek 官方产品。  ## 新增与改进  - **上游运行时升级**：从 `v0.1.5-rc.1` 升级至 `v0.1.5-rc.2`，同步更新桌面端依赖和适配补丁。 - **统一各平台打包方式**：macOS、Windows、Linux 的稳定版与测试版均取消 ASAR 归档，以普通目录存放应用资源。 - Windows 稳定版与测试版统一使用普通压缩级别。 - 补充打包后文件系统访问、目录元数据和内置 Cordis 技能发现、读取检查。  ## 修复  - 调整 Electron 启动配置与校验规则，使其匹配不使用 ASAR 的应用结构。 - 修正 Windows Portable ZIP 检查，使其验证新的应用目录结构。 - 同步更新 macOS 打包、发布和运行时检查，适配新的资源路径。  ## 下载  可从本页 Assets 下载：  - [macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/releases/download/v2.0.10/DSH.Desktop-2.0.10-universal.dmg) - [Windows x64](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/releases/download/v2.0.10/DSH-Desktop-2.0.10-x64-Setup.exe)  macOS 安装包内的应用已完成签名和 Apple 公证。  ## 使用提示  请先退出旧版应用再安装。本次使用的 DeepSeek Harness `v0.1.5-rc.2` 为上游候选发布版本。  [完整变更记录](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.9...v2.0.10) · [项目网站](https://dshdesktop.cn)  ---  # English  This release upgrades DeepSeek Harness to `v0.1.5-rc.2`, updates packaging across platforms, and improves checks for the packaged runtime and bundled skills. Stable and Beta include the same runtime upgrade and packaging improvements.  DSH Desktop is a community-maintained open-source project, not an official DeepSeek product.  ## New and improved  - **Upstream runtime upgrade:** Updated from `v0.1.5-rc.1` to `v0.1.5-rc.2`, along with desktop dependencies and compatibility patches. - **Consistent packaging across platforms:** Stable and Beta on macOS, Windows, and Linux now store application resources in ordinary directories instead of ASAR archives. - Aligned Windows Stable and Beta on normal compression. - Added checks for packaged file-system access, directory metadata, and discovery and reading of bundled Cordis skills.  ## Fixes  - Adjusted Electron launch configuration and validation rules to match the application structure without ASAR. - Corrected Windows Portable ZIP checks to validate the new application directory layout. - Updated macOS packaging, release, and runtime checks for the new resource paths.  ## Downloads  Download from Assets below:  - [macOS Universal · Apple Silicon / Intel](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/releases/download/v2.0.10/DSH.Desktop-2.0.10-universal.dmg) - [Windows x64](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/releases/download/v2.0.10/DSH-Desktop-2.0.10-x64-Setup.exe)  The application inside the macOS installer is signed and notarized by Apple.  ## Usage notes  Quit the previous version before installing. The bundled DeepSeek Harness `v0.1.5-rc.2` is an upstream release candidate.  [Full changelog](https://github.com/anywhere-labs/dsh-desktop/compare/v2.0.9...v2.0.10) · [Project website](https://dshdesktop.cn)
```

```
## 上下文进度条默认打开  输入框上方的分段上下文进度条现在**默认显示**，不用再去 `/settings → statusBar → contextBar` 打开。  - 以前显式关掉过的用户不受影响——设置优先，更新不会替你打开。 - 极简模式（minimal）依旧只保留 model + 工作目录，不会凭空多出进度条。  ## 超长单行不再拖慢转录  粘贴的巨型单行、工具调用里的一行大命令、压缩后的 JS、单行日志……任何**单行超过 1000 字符**的转录文本（user 消息、assistant 正文、工具卡标题与正文）都会在进入布局前折叠，行尾显示 `… 已折叠 N 字符（点击或 ctrl+o 展开）`。  - **鼠标点这一行**（工具卡点卡面）即可展开，再点一次收起；键盘用 `Ctrl+O`。 - 只有真被折叠的行可点：普通消息保持不可点，拖选复制不受影响；流式中的行不参与。 - 思考（thinking）行不变，仍是三行预览。 - 实测同一条 20 万字符单行：转录挂载 162ms → **4.5ms**，重渲染 12ms → **0.6ms**。  ## 其他  - 修好 `verify-display-settings` 里一条早已失效的断言（脚本此前跑不到后续用例），现为 29/29。 - 新增 `verify-long-line-fold` 回归（26 断言，含真实鼠标点击组）。   <!-- Release notes generated using configuration in .github/release.yml at v0.10.1 -->  ## What's Changed * refactor(adapter): Adapter 分层重构 by @T-Auto in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/705 * channel.ts的重构与解耦 by @T-Auto in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/776 * ci: run-ci-group 空分片判配置错误（#810 审查意见） by @Nagi-ovo in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/818 * fix(overlay): 浮层 maxHeight 钳到锚点上方真实空间，picker 窗口预算随之——短会话高终端下 /model 焦点行不可见（#493 #698） by @Nagi-ovo in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/817 * fix(render): 修复多行 user 置顶提示向转录左侧溢出（#828） by @ccch1mneyyy in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/829 * feat(transcript): 全屏转录支持 PgUp/PgDn 按页翻动消息列表 by @nicklin11 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/756 * fix: 适配 DSH 0.1.5-alpha.2，补齐 Session V3 与启动兼容 by @CikeSeven in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/831 * feat(skills): review 技能升级为全功能维护者审查（八阶段·五轴·deslop·契约快照） by @AdamPlatin123 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/774 * fix: 修复"/"呼出的命令面板异常高亮渲染的问题 by @FlameTN7 in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/819 * feat: 适配上游 DSH 0.1.5-rc.1（deepseek-flash 新默认模型） by @CikeSeven in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/843 * perf(adapter): ChannelUi 读投影直通 traceEvents，长会话不再每帧重建事件日志（#826） by @ccch1mneyyy in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/845 * feat: 上下文进度条默认开 + 超长单行折叠与鼠标交互（0.10.1） by @ccch1mneyyy in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/848  ## New Contributors * @nicklin11 made their first contribution in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/756 * @FlameTN7 made their first contribution in https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/pull/819  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/ccch1mneyyy/dsh-TUI/compare/v0.10.0...v0.10.1
```

### 2026-09-11T01:37:03Z [**briqt/moke**](https://github.com/briqt/moke)

```
**moke · 墨客 v0.1.20** — Android 原生 SSH / mosh 终端。   ### 新增 - **首次连接新主机时先确认指纹**（默认开启）。此前首连是静默记录指纹的：这一刻若被中间人截住，假指纹入库后每次校验都只会确认那把假密钥，而用户全程看不到任何提示。现在会把目标地址、密钥算法和 SHA256 指纹摆出来，核对后再点「信任」；取消则中止连接、不记录任何东西。指纹变更依旧一律拒绝（与此开关无关）。设置 → 终端与输入 → 「首次连接自动信任新主机」可以回到静默记录的旧行为。 - **主界面可以左右滑动切换「连接 · 会话 · 设置」**：页序与底栏一致，滑过半屏即切换，标题、底栏高亮与按钮同步跟上。底栏点击与返回键的行为不变；列表的长按拖动重排也不受影响（横向滑动切分区，长按之后才是拖动）。 - **「跳到底部」**：滑进历史深处后，终端右下角出现一个按钮，一下回到最新输出。此前回到底部只能反复往回滑，或者随便敲一个键——而敲键是真的把字节发给远端。  ### 修复 - **修复英文界面里仍出现中文的会话状态文案**（[#1](https://github.com/briqt/moke/issues/1)，感谢 @PuggNerd 的精确复现步骤）。两个原因：   - `[会话结束 (code N)]` 与 `[连接失败: …]` 是写死在终端内核里的中文——那一层拿不到应用资源，无论选什么语言都只会出中文。   - 更普遍的一层：连接提示、传输错误、通知这些由后台产生的文案，取字符串时跟随的是**系统**语言而不是应用内选择的语言。于是「系统中文 + 应用内选英文」时，界面是英文，这些文案却仍是中文（反之亦然）。现在它们一律跟随应用内语言，切换后立即生效，无需重启应用。 - 一并补齐其余漏翻：字体下载 / 导入失败的提示（无下载地址、sha256 校验失败、压缩包内找不到字体、文件不可读、不是有效字体）。 - **修复凭据无法解密时可能静默丢失全部连接**：连接凭据由本机密钥库里的密钥加密，而把备份恢复到新设备后那个密钥不在了。此前这会表现成「一台主机都没有」，接着任何一次写入都把原数据永久覆盖。现在这种情况会明确说明原因并拒绝写入；连接数据也不再进入系统自动备份，避免在新机上留下一份解不开的副本。加密失败时同样不再退回明文保存。 - **修复主机编辑可能被静默回滚**：会话开着时修改这台主机（名称、地址、端口、凭据、跳板机等），随后在该会话上「重新连接」或「复制会话」，改动会被还原成会话打开时的样子。同一原因还会吃掉「记住的 tmux 会话名」——于是选择器承诺的"下次连接直接进入"失效、每次连接都再问一遍。现在这类"顺手记一下"的字段（最近连接时间、tmux 会话名）按字段写入，不再整条覆盖。 - **修复关闭 mosh 会话后远端仍留着 mosh-server**：mosh 服务端按漫游语义设计，客户端消失只当作网络断了、会一直等下一个客户端。此前关闭会话只结束本地客户端，远端每次都留下一个进程和一个占用的 UDP 端口，直到主机重启；tmux 也会因此一直显示为「已附加」，面板里的客户端数不准。现在关闭会话会让客户端走 mosh 自己的退出流程通知服务端结束，并额外给服务端设了失联上限（24 小时），应用被强制停止这类情况下远端最终也会自己退出。远端 tmux 会话本身不受影响，照旧继续运行。 - **修复进入终端后外接键盘打不进字**：终端页打开时键盘焦点没交给终端本身，蓝牙/USB 键盘敲的内容全部落空，而第一次回车会触发顶栏的返回键、直接退出会话（软键盘不受影响，因为点击终端时会顺带取得焦点）。现在进入终端即取得键盘焦点，不再需要先点一下终端正文。 - **修复「全屏程序内滑动」的两档设置会漏到普通命令行**：选「始终发方向键」后，在 shell 提示符上滑动会去翻命令历史、把正在输入的命令换掉；选「始终发滚轮」则会把滚轮转义序列直接打进命令行。两种情况下本地滚屏历史还都彻底滚不动。这三档现在严格只在全屏程序内生效，普通命令行一律滚本地历史。 - **修复 tmux 接管滚屏时滑动过冲**：一次滑动就把整段历史穿掉，很难停在想看的位置。客户端已经按"手指走过几行"发出等量滚轮事件，而远端 tmux 默认每个事件再滚 5 行，叠加滑动惯性后就是十倍量级的过冲。现在远端每个滚轮事件只滚一行，滑动与内容 1:1 跟手；备用屏（less / man / vim）里的翻页也一并收敛成一行一次。 - **修复 tmux 附加状态被误判成「没附加上」**：附加确认在连接后 1 秒就问远端"这个 tmux 有几个客户端"，而 mosh 要先引导 mosh-server、再由启动命令里的 tmux 去附加，中间有几秒——第一次数到 0 就被当成结论。后果是一个其实已经附加成功的会话被永久标成未附加：面板给出「加入」（再开一个客户端而不是返回）、detach 只说「会话已结束」、tmux 接管滚屏的绑定也不会下发。现在只有确认窗口内**持续**为 0 才判定未附上；确认结果在会话结束之后才返回时也不再据此判定——detach 本身就会让客户端数归零。 - **修复 tmux 会话重连后不再核对是否真的附加上**：点「重新连接」后，之后 detach 会被显示成「会话已结束」；万一实际没附加上（远端缺 tmux 或终端类型不可用），顶栏还会继续把它标成 tmux 会话。现在重连与首次附加一样会核对。 - **修复离开 tmux 后终端里仍写「会话结束」**：底部提示写的是「已离开 tmux，远端会话仍在运行」，屏幕上却写「会话结束」，两句自相矛盾。现在附加在 tmux 上的会话正常 detach 后，终端里写的是「已离开 tmux」；普通会话的文案不变。 - **修复从会话列表关闭 tmux 会话时提示可能不完整**：刚从选择器新建的会话，关闭确认里不会说明远端 tmux 及其中程序会继续运行。 - **修复上传成功后看不到刚传上去的文件**：远端文件列表不会自动刷新，底部传输条还显示着上一次传输的「已完成」，看着像上传没发生。现在上传完成会刷新正在看的那个目录，传输条也改为显示最近那一条。 - **修复文件传输队列可能被一次异常卡死**：出错后队列不再停在「排队中」直到重启应用，而是把该任务标记为失败并继续后面的任务。 - **修复会话列表长按拖动手柄重排时会同时弹出「修改标题」**：改名的长按此前挂在整张卡片上、把拖动手柄一起盖住，于是一次长按同时触发拖动和改名，重排全程都被弹窗打断。现在改名的长按只作用在卡片的文字区，手柄只管拖动；单击卡片任意处进入会话不变。  ### 优化 - **大幅减少 mosh 主机上的 SSH 登录次数**：mosh 下管理 tmux 需要一条 SSH 控制连接，此前探测、附加确认、滚动绑定各自新建一条，进一次会话可能产生十几次登录（在服务器日志里刷屏，有登录频率限制的主机还可能因此把自己挡在门外）。现在忙时复用同一条、空闲后自动断开；连接不上时的重试也改为指数退避。 - **已经探明的 tmux 状态不再重复探测**：从文件页返回、来回切换会话不再每次都去问一遍远端。 - **SSH 连接期间显示「正在连接 …」**：此前建连与认证的这几秒里终端一片空白，分不清是在连还是卡住了。 - **后台保活通知的授权改到第一次开会话时才问**：此前冷启动就弹窗，那时用户还没连过任何主机，无从判断这个权限是做什么用的；而这条通知只在有会话时才存在。 - **会话列表可以一次清理已结束的会话**：存在已结束会话时列表顶部给出「清理已结束 (N)」，不必一条条关。 - **tmux 会话数角标改用中性色**：原来是告警红，看着像未读消息或出错，其实只是"远端有几个 tmux 会话可以进"。 - **字体说明在英文界面也能看到**（此前仅中文界面显示），包括大体积字体的下载提示。 - 新增一条构建期检查：产品代码里再出现面向用户的中文字面量就会让单元测试失败，这类漏翻不再依赖人工核对。   ### 安装（两个包择一） - `moke-v0.1.20.apk` — 常规包，中文回退用内置思源黑体子集，体积小。 - `moke-v0.1.20-maple.apk` — 自带 Maple Mono 并设为默认中文回退（开箱中英等宽），包更大。  允许"安装未知来源应用"后安装（稳定签名，可覆盖升级）。  ### 特性 - 多会话，原生终端渲染（Apache-2.0 terminal-view 内核） - SSH（密码 / 私钥）+ mosh（UDP 漫游） - 实时预览外观 · 多套配色 · 字体管理（主字体 + 中文回退） - 连接分组排序 · 双排附加键 · 文本段输入  本版改动见 [CHANGELOG](https://github.com/briqt/moke/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md)。   **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/briqt/moke/compare/v0.1.19...v0.1.20 
```

### 2026-09-13T09:59:07Z [**AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm**](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm)

```
### English  OxideTerm 2.0.29 expands OxideSens with more AI providers, resumable task execution, complete persistent history, and conversation archiving. It also improves terminal responsiveness and command observation, reduces editor memory and background work, and adds independent IDE typography settings.  #### 🤖 AI Providers and Task Execution  - Added OpenAI Responses API support with stateless history replay, including restoration of the conversation context needed for subsequent requests. - Added a native xAI Grok provider with model discovery and model capability settings. - Updated the recommended DeepSeek Flash model to `deepseek-flash`, with matching context-window and reasoning-level support. Existing Flash aliases remain recognized, and `deepseek-v4-pro` keeps its current name; saved custom model lists are not rewritten. - Added task checkpoints and resumable execution, bounded condition waits, dependency-aware read scheduling, and classified recovery for interrupted or failed operations. - Replaced manual context and response limits with automatic budgeting, and improved handling of follow-up instructions during running tasks. - Added clarification questions during execution. An agent can present a question, wait for the user's answer, and continue the task.  #### 💬 Complete Chat History and Archiving  - Removed the conversation message-count cap. Persistent history retains messages, tool activity, subagent history, and alternative branches rather than discarding older messages at a fixed limit. - Reworked history storage around incremental writes, separately stored metadata, chunked content, and on-demand message loading. Large conversations no longer require all message bodies to be loaded for browsing the conversation list. - Added conversation archiving, an archived-conversation list, and restore actions. Archiving hides a conversation from the normal list while retaining its history; archiving alone does not cancel running work. - Added separate indexed pagination for active and archived conversations. Startup loads the first 50 active conversations; archived conversations load when their list is opened, with additional pages available on demand. Clearing all conversations still includes histories outside the loaded pages. - Fixed loading failures involving older history metadata. Existing history is migrated to the new store while the original database is retained. Conversations written after migration are stored in the new database and are not copied back to the old version's database. - Added regression coverage for archive and restore persistence, metadata pagination, message preservation, and conversation switching without losing drafts.  #### 🖱️ Chat Display and Scrolling  - Added a chat scrollbar and improved scroll-position preservation when reading earlier messages, reducing jumps back to the current screen during content updates. - Fixed a list-rendering borrow conflict that could crash the application during tool activity and history loading. - Simplified tool activity presentation, removing internal activity navigation, JSON-path controls, and redundant “more content” rows. Messages display their complete activity content automatically. - Fixed stopped or historical tool calls remaining in a spinning state, preventing continued animation and unnecessary redraws after the operation ended. - Adjusted conversation-list heading typography and selected-row corners so the highlight follows the list's actual boundaries.  #### 🛠️ AI and Terminal Command Handling  - Removed the recurring manual terminal-control handback gate. Tool request ownership is released when the request ends, while stale requests cannot release ownership belonging to a newer operation. - Improved command completion, cancellation, and owned-process cleanup. Stopping observation does not claim that an already-issued remote command has stopped. - Improved output capture on shells without integration events. Stable output can be returned as a captured snapshot without being reported as a confirmed command exit. - Kept AI command tracking functional when visual command marks are disabled. - Kept privilege-authentication input local to the running command and improved isolation from ordinary terminal input handling.  #### ⚡ Terminal, Connections, and Remote Desktop  - Moved SSH terminal parsing to an owned background worker, reduced output copies, and bounded batch-flush latency to reduce UI work during continuous output. - Fixed switching away from a terminal incorrectly marking already-displayed output as unread, including the first switch after opening a terminal. - Fixed the command sender retaining keyboard focus after clicking a terminal pane. - Improved RDP text pasting through acknowledged clipboard transfers. - Added default connection names when editing unnamed saved connections. - Fixed detached-tab cleanup after native window closure on Windows.  #### ✍️ IDE Mini and Editor Performance  - Added independent IDE font-family, Chinese/CJK fallback-font, and font-weight settings alongside the existing font-size and line-height controls. Editor settings can continue to follow the terminal defaults or use explicit overrides. - Shared immutable document text across editor snapshots and save requests, and reclaimed unused text storage to reduce retained memory. - Moved cancellable syntax work off the UI thread and avoided redundant queued work for superseded document versions. - Reused syntax blocks, updated presentation indexes locally, and reduced temporary highlight copies and repeated derived calculations. - Fixed editor callback ownership cycles and made caret-blink scheduling cancel correctly when the editor loses focus or is released.  #### ⌨️ Shortcuts and Validation  - Exposed context-specific and plugin shortcuts in settings, and fixed old shortcuts remaining registered after runtime updates. - Expanded mixed-language terminal benchmark coverage and recorded SSH background-parsing measurements. - Fixed CI formatting and asynchronous scheduler issues, and adjusted large-storage test timeout guards without reducing their data-preservation assertions. - Updated affected interface text across all 11 supported languages. Focused archive, pagination, draft-preservation, and storage regression checks passed; the maintainer completed GUI validation before approving this release.  ### 中文  OxideTerm 2.0.29 为 OxideSens 增加了更多模型接入、可恢复任务执行、完整历史持久化和对话归档，同时改善终端响应与命令输出观察，降低编辑器内存占用和后台计算开销，并新增独立的 IDE 字体设置。  #### 🤖 AI 模型接入与任务执行  - 新增 OpenAI Responses API 支持及无状态历史重放，恢复后续请求所需的对话上下文。 - 新增原生 xAI Grok 提供商，支持模型发现及模型能力设置。 - 将 DeepSeek Flash 推荐模型名更新为 `deepseek-flash`，同步上下文容量与思考档位识别。旧 Flash 别名仍可识别，`deepseek-v4-pro` 保持原名；已保存的自定义模型列表不会被重写。 - 新增任务检查点和可恢复执行、有界条件等待、按依赖安排的读取调度，以及中断或失败操作的分类恢复。 - 使用自动预算管理替代手动上下文与回复限制，并改善任务运行期间追加指令的处理。 - 新增执行中的澄清提问：代理可以提出问题、等待用户回答，再继续任务。  #### 💬 完整聊天历史与归档  - 移除对话消息数量上限，持久化保留消息、工具活动、子代理历史和替代分支，不再按固定条数丢弃较早消息。 - 重构历史存储，采用增量写入、独立元数据、内容分块和消息按需加载；浏览对话列表时不再需要加载大对话的全部正文。 - 新增对话归档、已归档列表和恢复操作。归档后对话从普通列表隐藏，历史记录继续保留；归档本身不会取消正在执行的任务。 - 普通与归档对话分别使用索引分页。启动只加载首批 50 条普通对话，归档列表打开时才读取，后续按需加载更多。清空全部对话仍包含尚未加载的历史记录。 - 修复旧历史元数据导致的加载失败。现有历史会迁移到新存储，同时保留原数据库；迁移后产生的对话记录写入新数据库，不会回写到旧版本使用的数据库。 - 补充归档与恢复持久化、元数据分页、消息保留，以及切换对话时保留草稿的回归验证。  #### 🖱️ 聊天展示与滚动  - 新增聊天滚动条，改善向上阅读历史时的滚动位置保持，减少内容更新导致的回跳。 - 修复工具活动和历史加载期间，列表渲染借用冲突可能引发的应用崩溃。 - 简化工具活动展示，移除内部活动翻页、JSON 路径控件及重复的“更多内容”行，自动展示消息的完整活动内容。 - 修复已停止或历史工具调用仍持续转圈的问题，避免操作结束后继续动画和无效刷新。 - 调整对话列表标题字号和选中行圆角，使高亮符合列表实际边界。  #### 🛠️ AI 与终端命令处理  - 移除反复要求手动交还终端控制权的阻塞机制。工具请求结束后释放该请求的控制权，旧请求不会错误释放新操作持有的控制权。 - 改善命令完成、取消和所属进程的清理处理；停止观察不会被表述为已经停止远端命令。 - 改善缺少 Shell 集成事件时的输出捕获。稳定输出可以作为已捕获快照返回，不会被误报为已确认的命令退出。 - 关闭可视命令标记后，AI 命令跟踪仍可正常工作。 - 特权认证输入保持在正在运行的命令中处理，并改善其与普通终端输入处理的隔离。  #### ⚡ 终端、连接与远程桌面  - 将 SSH 终端解析移到具有明确生命周期的后台工作线程，减少输出复制并限制批量刷新延迟，降低连续输出时的界面线程开销。 - 修复切出终端后，已展示输出被误判为未读的问题，包括终端打开后的第一次切换。 - 修复点击终端窗格后，命令发送栏仍占用键盘焦点的问题。 - 通过带确认的剪贴板传输改善 RDP 文本粘贴。 - 编辑未命名的已保存连接时，自动提供默认名称。 - 修复 Windows 原生窗口关闭后的分离标签清理问题。  #### ✍️ IDE Mini 与编辑器性能  - 在现有字号、行高设置基础上，新增独立的 IDE 字体、中文/CJK 回退字体和字重设置，可继续跟随终端默认值，也可单独覆盖。 - 编辑器快照与保存请求共享不可变文档文本，并回收不再使用的文本存储，减少常驻内存。 - 将可取消的语法分析移出界面线程，避免为已经过期的文档版本重复排队计算。 - 复用语法块、局部更新展示索引，减少临时高亮复制与重复派生计算。 - 修复编辑器回调所有权循环，使光标闪烁任务在失焦或编辑器释放时正确取消。  #### ⌨️ 快捷键与验证  - 设置中展示上下文专用快捷键和插件快捷键，修复运行时更新后旧快捷键仍被注册的问题。 - 扩充混合语言终端基准覆盖，并记录 SSH 后台解析的测量结果。 - 修复 CI 格式检查和异步调度问题，调整大型存储测试的超时保护，同时保留数据完整性断言。 - 相关界面文案同步维护全部 11 种语言。归档、分页、草稿保留及存储的聚焦回归检查通过；维护者已完成 GUI 验收并批准发布。  ## 📥 Download for your system  | Operating system | x64 | ARM64 | |---|---|---| | **Windows** | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_windows_x64-setup.exe) | [Setup (.exe)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_windows_arm64-setup.exe) | | **macOS** | [DMG (Intel)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_macos_x64.dmg) | [DMG (Apple Silicon)](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_macos_arm64.dmg) | | **Linux** | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_linux_x64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_linux_x64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_linux_x64.rpm) | [AppImage](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_linux_arm64.AppImage) · [DEB](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_linux_arm64.deb) · [RPM](https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/releases/download/v2.0.29/OxideTerm_2.0.29_linux_arm64.rpm) |  Portable archives, signatures, and `sha256sums.txt` remain available in the release assets below.  <details> <summary>📌 Installation Tips / 安装提示</summary>  ### macOS  Downloaded `.dmg` files may be quarantined by Gatekeeper. Run in Terminal:  bash xattr -cr ~/Downloads/OxideTerm_*.dmg # or after install / 或安装后 xattr -cr /Applications/OxideTerm.app   ### Windows  If SmartScreen warns, click **More info** -> **Run anyway**.  若 SmartScreen 弹出警告，点击 **更多信息** -> **仍要运行**。  ### Linux  bash # AppImage chmod +x OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage && ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.AppImage  # Debian/Ubuntu sudo dpkg -i OxideTerm_*_linux_*.deb && sudo apt-get install -f  # Fedora/RHEL-compatible systems sudo dnf install ./OxideTerm_*_linux_*.rpm   </details>  ## 🔗 Links  - Documentation: https://oxideterm.app - GitHub Issues: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/issues - Changelog: https://github.com/AnalyseDeCircuit/oxideterm/blob/main/.github/release-notes/stable-changelog.md
```

### 2026-09-15T19:10:49Z [**t8y2/dbx**](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx)

```
### 新功能  - 插件市场支持通过 dbx:// 深度链接直接拉起桌面端安装插件包 - MongoDB 支持 estimatedDocumentCount 估算集合文档数量，并支持 db.stats 与 db.serverStatus 服务器状态查询 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#9233](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9233)) - MongoDB CSV 导入支持编辑自动推断的列类型 (contributed by @onenewcode) (PR [#9102](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9102)) - AI 助手支持重命名会话并增强数据库选择 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#9228](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9228)) - 数据比较支持一键重新比较，并清晰区分源库与目标库 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9215](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9215)) - Ctrl+F 搜索支持按当前焦点区域路由到对应的搜索框 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9209](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9209)) - 查询消息支持跳转到 PostgreSQL 报错位置 (contributed by @amwps290) (PR [#9184](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9184)) - 侧边栏本地表搜索支持匹配表注释 (contributed by @CHNHJF) (PR [#9146](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9146))  ### 改进  - 优化插件中心列表展示，新增网格/列表视图切换、描述悬浮提示与主页快捷入口 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9220](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9220)) - 插件市场卡片新增官方认证标识，并优化操作按钮展示 - 插件宿主新增 host.saveFile 桥接能力，支持插件触发文件保存 - 优化自动更新下载策略，检测到新版本时自动替换已下载的过期安装包，并将更新通知开关迁移至设置 - 优化查询结果序列化路径，减少大结果集内存拷贝，提升查询响应速度 - 完善 DuckDB 的 SQL 关键字补全 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9214](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9214))  ### 修复  - 修复 PostgreSQL 系数据库建表因连接中断误报失败的问题，现会核实表是否已实际创建 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9221](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9221)) - 修复 PostgreSQL 导出 DDL 时外键未限定 Schema、级联删表语句不完整的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9178](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9178)) - 修复 PostgreSQL 无结果集语句的 NOTICE 消息不显示的问题 (contributed by @amwps290) (PR [#9142](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9142)) - 修复 Oracle 数值列查询结果不可编辑的问题 (contributed by @onenewcode) (PR [#9143](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9143)) - 修复 Oracle SQL 中 # 号标识符被截断的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9206](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9206)) - 修复 MongoDB find/aggregate 后无法链式调用 .toArray() 与 .pretty() 的问题 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#9103](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9103)) - 修复 MongoDB 执行失败时仅提示通用命令信息的问题，现展示具体错误 (contributed by @thailoc-dev) (PR [#9155](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9155)) - 修复 OceanBase Oracle 模式下无法手动控制事务的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#8084](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/8084)) - 修复 GaussDB MERGE 语句范围识别不完整的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9203](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9203)) - 修复 SQL Server 存储过程消息被当作结果集展示的问题，现统一显示在消息区域 - 修复 SQL Server 导出脚本中自增列 INSERT 未包装 IDENTITY_INSERT 的问题 (contributed by @CHNHJF) (PR [#9182](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9182)) - 修复 DB2 遇到非法字符数据时报错中断的问题，现自动容错解码 (contributed by @bigdogcome) (PR [#9194](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9194)) - 修复 GBase 8s 标识符被误加反引号导致执行失败的问题 (contributed by @yiqiui) (PR [#9151](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9151)) - 修复 Cassandra SQL 补全误提示表别名的问题 (contributed by @thanhthai3010) (PR [#9136](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9136)) - 修复 Redis 本地键搜索在粘贴完整键名时结果异常的问题 - 修复 MySQL VARBINARY 单元格中 GBK 编码文本预览乱码的问题 - 修复数据网格中未提交的新增行在列筛选后不可见的问题 (contributed by @eryajf) (PR [#9230](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9230)) - 修复数据网格重新加载前未刷新表元数据导致列信息过期的问题 (contributed by @soddygo) (PR [#9156](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9156)) - 修复转置表格视图中列跳转失效的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9195](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9195)) - 修复复制查询结果生成 INSERT 语句时列别名丢失的问题 - 修复刷新连接时对象缓存未等待失效导致元数据展示过期的问题 (contributed by @soddygo) (PR [#9114](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9114)) - 修复独立 JRE 离线包无法导入的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9204](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9204)) - 修复支持 Schema 的数据库生成 SQL 时未遵循“包含数据库名”设置的问题 (contributed by @CHNHJF) (PR [#9123](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9123)) - 修复新建视图未遵循标识符引号设置的问题 (contributed by @CHNHJF) (PR [#9145](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9145)) - 修复焦点位于连接列表空白区域时 Ctrl+F 无法定位到侧边栏搜索框的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9234](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9234)) - 修复快捷键捕获输入框在中文占位符下宽度不足显示不全的问题 (contributed by @Abeautifulsnow) (PR [#9192](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9192)) - 修复恢复默认快捷键后仍停留在快捷键捕获状态的问题 (contributed by @Abeautifulsnow) (PR [#9158](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9158)) - 修复 macOS 下 Cmd+H 隐藏窗口快捷键被替换的问题 (contributed by @Abeautifulsnow) (PR [#9129](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9129)) - 修复工具栏在宽度阈值附近拖拽时闪烁的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9172](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9172)) - 修复垂直标签栏下插件标签页布局错乱的问题 - 修复拖拽调整面板大小时经过插件内嵌页面卡顿的问题 - 修复 AI 对话中连续长内容不换行溢出的问题 (contributed by @zipg) (PR [#9227](https://github.com/t8y2/dbx/pull/9227))  ### 赞助信息  - Jalapeno Cloud 限时折扣🎉：GLM 5.3 -> 八折、DeepSeek V4 Flash 0731 -> 七折（9 月 16 日至 9 月 25 日），[福利入口](https://www.jalapeno-cloud.ai/DBX)  --- <!-- dbx-cnb-mirror --> > 国内下载：如果 GitHub 下载较慢，可从 [CNB 镜像](https://cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx/-/releases/tag/v0.6.14) 下载桌面端安装包，Docker 镜像从 `docker.cnb.cool/dbxio.com/dbx:v0.6.14` 拉取。
```

### 2026-09-15T11:15:59Z [**razvandimescu/numa**](https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa)

```
A patch release with a security fix for forward mode, fewer false DNSSEC failures in strict mode, and a fix for a 0.23.0 regression in recursive mode.  ## 🔒 Security  - Forward mode now sends upstreams a random query ID instead of passing the client's through, closing a one-packet cache-poisoning path (#397) - rustls bumped to 0.23.45 for RUSTSEC-2026-0285 (#396)  ## 🧭 Resolution  - Recursive mode resolves names like `multi.surbl.org` again, fixing a 0.23.0 regression where replies naming the root were rejected (#387, reported in #386) - A cached SERVFAIL now lives for seconds instead of `min_ttl`, so one upstream hiccup no longer blackholes a domain for a minute (#388, #376) - NXDOMAIN takes its TTL from the authority SOA, per RFC 2308 (#389)  ## 🚦 New limit on concurrent lookups  `max_concurrent_resolutions` (default 512, `0` disables) caps cache misses running at once across UDP, TCP, DoT and DoH. Cached, local and coalesced answers are never counted. Over the cap, UDP is dropped silently and stream transports get SERVFAIL. (#364)  **If you run numa for many clients, watch `queries.refused` and `resolutions` in `/stats` after upgrading.**  ## 🔏 DNSSEC  - Strict mode no longer SERVFAILs zones such as `isc.org` and `.ai`: RSA keys under 2048 bits verify, RSASHA512 and ECDSA P-384 are supported, and unsupported algorithms are treated as Insecure (#390, #393) - Denial-of-existence proofs are authenticated (#393) - DS queries go to the parent side of the zone cut, and signed RRsets are ordered by RDATA when rebuilt (#391, #392)  ## ⚙️ Operations  - numa exits cleanly on SIGTERM and SIGINT, so `docker stop` works (#368) - `numa lan|block|dnssec on|off` edits the config the running daemon loaded, not a `numa.toml` in the current directory. Thanks @hcars (#371) - ODoH errors now say whether the relay or the target failed, and the target config fetch is bounded by the query budget (#369, #377) - The ODoH relay answers unknown paths and a trailing slash on `/relay` with a usable response instead of an empty 404 (#369, #365)  ## Also notable  - Chinese README, first contribution from @Lkhsss (#268) - Recipe for local DNS records and split DNS (#370) - Docker image builds again from a tag (#363) - Benchmark tooling fixes and a tag-baseline script (#361, #379)  <details> <summary>All changes</summary>  ## What's Changed * fix(bench): unbreak the comparison modes, add tag-baseline tooling by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/361 * fix(docker): copy build.rs into the builder image by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/363 * fix(serve): handle SIGTERM and SIGINT so containers can stop numa by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/368 * docs(recipes): add local DNS records and split DNS recipe by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/370 * Fix Linux path resolution by @hcars in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/371 * chore(deps): bump actions/checkout from 4 to 7 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/374 * chore(deps): bump the minor-and-patch group with 11 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/373 * chore(deps): bump rust from 1.97-alpine to 1.98-alpine in the minor-and-patch group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/372 * fix(odoh): bound the target config fetch by the query budget by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/377 * fix(bench): use real key material in dnssec verify benches by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/379 * test(relay): cover a real forward end to end by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/380 * fix(odoh): name the hop that produced a failed ODoH status by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/369 * Adding README in Chinese by @Lkhsss in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/268 * feat(admission): cap concurrent cache-miss resolutions across transports by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/364 * fix(wire): parse the root name as "." so root UDP replies match their query by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/387 * fix(cache): cap a cached resolution failure at seconds, not min_ttl by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/388 * fix(dnssec): verify RSA keys below 2048 bits, add RSASHA512 and ECDSA P-384 by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/390 * chore(deps): bump rustls to 0.23.45 for RUSTSEC-2026-0285 by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/396 * fix(forward): send upstream a random TXID, not the client's by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/397 * fix(dnssec): order RRsets by RDATA alone when rebuilding signed data by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/391 * fix(recursive): send DS queries to the parent side of the zone cut by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/392 * fix(dnssec): treat unsupported algorithms as Insecure and authenticate denial proofs by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/393 * fix(cache): take the negative TTL from the authority SOA by @razvandimescu in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/389  ## New Contributors * @hcars made their first contribution in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/371 * @Lkhsss made their first contribution in https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa/pull/268  **Full Changelog**: ### 2026-09-15T04:01:16Z [**JuliusBrussee/caveman**](https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman)
```

```
Caveman 2.7.0 installs natively into more agents, makes uninstall clean up what it wired, stops Codex approval rules from breaking, and ships the hosted proxy mode and framework middleware on the bin-v1.1.7 runtime.  Highlights: - Install: agents that read physical skill directories now get a native, ownership-tracked copy. `--uninstall` disables native agent integrations and says when a native route survives. The standalone installer is safe on JSONC and foreign manifests, and `caveman-init --force` refreshes an installed rule file. The update path is documented. - Plugin: the Claude Code plugin and Gemini extension manifests declare the release version, so hosts stop showing a commit sha or a years-old `1.0.1` (#321, #403). - Skill: an explicit reply-language instruction wins over the conversation's dominant language. Level switches carry the right ruleset on both hosts, natural-language activation has fewer false positives, and the fallback ruleset no longer carries a language few-shot. - Stats: `/caveman-stats` reports only what the transcript shows; the unmeasured savings claims are retired from docs. - Codex: the API-key route gets the `/v1` prefix Codex appends to, and shell commands are no longer rewritten (that broke saved approval rules). `caveman wrap` stops registering a Codex shrink hook the README says it skips. - CLI: new `caveman stats` command surface; a wrapped host's endpoint is proven before its credentials are rerouted; agent config homes are honored; a loopback gateway stays out of a corporate proxy; `think.shrink` is honored by the native install. - opencode: mode changes are recorded. - Compress / shrink: the compress skill CLI runs as a plain script; shrink leaves mixed CJK strings byte-identical (#575) and escalates to SIGKILL when the upstream ignores the signal. - Pi: provider routing is gated on the live listener, with per-model originals and compat-mount routing for custom providers (#972). - Proxy (bin-v1.1.7): hosted mode (inbound token, AWS credential chain, container image at `ghcr.io/juliusbrussee/caveman-proxy`), framework middleware routes, byte-exact token-counting routes, per-request accounting with a local stats report, tighter secret redaction and SSRF policy, Bedrock SigV4 callers never re-signed as another principal, and recovery-store locking fixes.  Artifacts: - Signed binaries: bin-v1.1.7 - CLI: `npm install -g @caveman-ai/cli@1.3.4` (npm publish pending) - Pi extension: `@caveman-ai/pi@0.1.1` (npm publish pending) - MCP shrink proxy: `caveman-shrink@0.1.1` (npm publish pending) - SDKs: `@caveman-ai/sdk@1.1.0`, `caveman-sdk==1.1.0` - Middleware (alpha): `@caveman-ai/middleware@0.1.0-alpha.2`, `caveman-middleware==0.1.0a1`  Validation: - Installer suite (`node --test tests/installer/*.test.mjs`): 322 passed, 0 failed, including the bootstrap pin lockstep and host manifest version guards. - `tests/verify_repo.py`: all local verification checks passed. - CLI suite (94 runtime files): 1052 passed, 0 failed. - Pi extension suite: 117 passed. caveman-shrink suite: 28 passed. - bin-v1.1.7: all 38 release assets downloadable anonymously. - Nightly agent conformance: every pinned profile and the real compression engine green except Hermes, whose upstream package currently refuses to build a wheel. 
```

```
This image-focused update improves watermark removal on supported Gemini originals:  - Fixes skipped or misplaced removal on some 2752×1536 illustrated images. - Reduces the prominent residual on the reported cabinet image while preserving its wood grain and horizontal boundary. - Preserves existing content protection and best-effort output behavior.  Fine watermark contours may remain on some images. This update does not expand video support or guarantee removal from screenshots, edited images, or repeatedly watermarked inputs.  Distribution verified: npm SDK, GitHub assets, the official website's actual image-processing Worker, userscript and extension fallback download are all 1.0.43. The three reported images were re-tested on the deployed website Worker.  The 1.0.43 Chrome Web Store update has been manually submitted for review, as confirmed by the publisher on 2026-09-10. Approval and public availability are still pending; the last verified public version was 1.0.42. Use the updated website or manual extension package to try these fixes now.
```

### 2026-09-12T06:58:36Z [**tw93/Kaku**](https://github.com/tw93/Kaku)

```
<div align="center">   <img src="https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tw93/Kaku/main/assets/logo.png" alt="Kaku Logo" width="120" height="120" />   <h1 style="margin: 12px 0 6px;">Kaku V0.20.0</h1>   <p><em>A fast, out-of-the-box terminal built for AI coding.</em></p> </div>  ### Changelog 1. **Window Behavior**: Native macOS dragging and edge tiling work again, closing windows no longer triggers a delayed repaint crash, and new windows skip repeated waits after graphics initialization fails. 2. **Shell Integration**: Returning to the prompt clears stray mouse reports after a TUI exits unexpectedly, tmux keeps its prompt and Smart Tab, and fish grep completion expands correctly. 3. **AI Model Selection**: Switching providers no longer reuses the previous provider's model list, all returned models are shown, and manually configured models remain available. 4. **AI Chat**: Chats include local project type information without probing local projects during remote sessions, and cancellation interrupts retry waits promptly. 5. **Context Menu**: Right-click to paste, search, open AI chat, or manage panes, with an option in Settings to show the new-tab button. 6. **Theme Settings**: Standalone configurations can use the built-in light and dark themes, Settings saves to the custom configuration used at launch, manual color overrides are shown explicitly, and Fancy tabs use the selected theme. 7. **Link Detection**: Unrelated text after a hard newline stays out of link targets while automatically wrapped URLs keep their complete addresses. 8. **Version Reporting**: The GUI executable reports its package version without initializing a window.  ### 更新日志 1. **窗口操作**：恢复 macOS 原生拖动和边缘平铺，修复关闭窗口后可能出现的崩溃，图形初始化失败后新窗口不再重复等待。 2. **Shell 集成**：修复 TUI 异常退出后鼠标移动产生乱码的问题，保留 tmux 中的提示符和智能补全，并修正 fish 的 grep 补全展开。 3. **AI 模型选择**：切换服务后不再沿用旧服务的模型列表，完整展示服务返回的模型，并保留手动配置的模型。 4. **AI 对话**：补充当前项目类型信息，远程会话不读取本机项目，取消请求时不再等待重试倒计时结束。 5. **右键菜单**：支持粘贴、搜索、AI 对话和分屏操作，也可以在设置中开启新建标签页按钮。 6. **主题设置**：独立配置也能使用内置深浅主题，通过自定义配置启动时设置会保存到对应文件，手动配色覆盖主题时会显示说明，Fancy 标签栏也会跟随主题配色。 7. **链接识别**：避免把换行后的无关输出拼进链接，保留自动折行网址的完整地址。 8. **版本查询**：图形程序的版本查询无需初始化窗口即可正确返回。  Special thanks to @elonnzhang, @trxuan, and @yansigit for their contributions to this release.  > https://github.com/tw93/Kaku
```

### 2026-09-15T11:07:54Z [**lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager**](https://github.com/lbjlaq/Antigravity-Manager)

```
-   **[OpenAI / Codex 适配] 修复 Codex 客户端中 Gemini 模型思考过程未作为 reasoning summary 显示的问题 (PR #3439, Issue #3438)**:     -   **标准化 Reasoning Summary 事件**: 使用流式 `POST /v1/responses` 时，将 Gemini 的 `thought: true` 思考分片调整为标准 `rs_...` 项（`type: reasoning`），并通过 `response.reasoning_summary_part.*` 与 `response.reasoning_summary_text.*` 规范事件流输出，使 Codex 可以在合适位置正规渲染思考摘要。     -   **生命周期隔离与流结束闭合**: 在普通文本或工具调用开始前以及流结束时及时闭合 reasoning summary，确保与普通输出项的生命周期互不重叠，并保证会话持久化及 `response.completed` 输出一致性。 -   **[Prompt 格式规范与内存同步] 全局系统提示词换行隔离、Gemini 包装防重与配置即时生效 (PR #3433)**:     -   **Markdown 格式安全隔离**: 在 Antigravity 预置身份末尾及全局提示词后添加规范换行分隔符（`\n\n`），防止用户填写的 Markdown 标题紧贴前置粗体词造成渲染解析异常，并避免后续 HTTP 头部紧挨 Prompt 尾部。     -   **Gemini Wrapper 提示词去重**: 在 `wrap_request_v2` 中增加去重检测，防止请求在多次包装或重试时反复注入全局系统提示词。     -   **配置保存即时同步全局内存**: 在 `save_config` 中将全局内存配置（Thinking Budget、全局系统提示词、图片思考模式、上下文压缩参数等）更新提取至代理实例检查之外，确保反代服务未启动或处于停止状态时，保存的配置也能立即同步到内存中。 -   **[存储与数据管理] 支持自定义数据存储目录并一键平滑全量迁移 (Issue #3441)**:     -   **自定义数据目录与自举寻址机制**: 针对默认存储在 C 盘（`~/.antigravity_tools`）导致系统盘容易爆满的问题，新增持久化指针文件寻址。支持在高级设置中自由选择任意磁盘目录（如 `D:\AntigravityData`），应用启动时自动识别并重定向。     -   **全量平滑迁移与空间安全释放**: 在高级设置中提供「更改并迁移」功能，一键将现有账号、全局配置、请求日志及 SQLite 数据库无损复制到新目录，支持迁移成功后自动清理原目录以释放 C 盘空间，并自动重启应用无缝加载新目录。 
```

```
## Install  bash uv tool install specify-cli --from git+https://github.com/github/spec-kit.git@v1.0.7 specify init my-project   ## Upcoming Deprecation The core `/speckit.taskstoissues` command is planned to move out of Spec Kit core in a future release as GitHub-specific issue tracking transitions to a bundled `github` extension. The command remains supported for now  ## What's Changed  - chore: bump version to 1.0.7 - fix(integrations): dispatch bob commands via `bob run` (#4492) - Bound speckit.clarify planning deferral to implementation details (#4507) - docs: simplify README around three processes (#4591) - chore(deps): bump actions/deploy-pages from 5.0.0 to 5.0.1 (#4517) - feat: add artifact-owned contribution lookup (#4550) - feat: expose hook contributions via specify artifact (#4348) - Update DocGuard — CDD Enforcement extension to v0.34.9 (#4545) - [extension] Update MAQA — Multi-Agent & Quality Assurance extension to v0.3.1 (#4544) - feat: add `specify artifact` introspection (#4305) - fix: require explicit refresh for bundle manifest changes (#4477) - fix(workflows): harden community submission workflows (#4510) - [extension] Add GitHub Issue Triage extension to community catalog (#4539) - Fix catalog-latest-url-bypass: require tag-pinned catalog download URLs (#4194) - refactor(workflows): let the evaluator report its own leaves (#4274) (#4460) - [preset] Add Secure Development Assurance Governance preset (#4513) - Add Product Definition as Code (PDaC) extension to community catalog (#4514) - feat: add JSON output to preset and extension lists (#4218) - docs: exempt repository maintenance workflows from PR throttle (#4499) - chore: refresh bug-assess workflow with gh-aw v0.88.7 (#4497) - chore: release 1.0.6, begin 1.0.7.dev0 development (#4511)
```

### 2026-09-15T06:37:23Z [**Huanshere/VideoLingo**](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo)

```
Changelog - Version 3.0.4  This release brings together the changes since v3.0.1, including the work previously labeled 3.0.2 and 3.0.3 in the source tree without separate release tags. It adds installation and interface improvements, persistent transcription reuse, dependency and deployment updates, and targeted fixes for subtitle timing and dubbing audio processing.  🚀 New Features  - Set up with uv using a project-local or shared environment. The staged installer can be rerun, and OneKeyStart checks the environment before launch, repairs missing/outdated dependencies, and records logs. `--check-only` checks without repairing or launching (#532, #537, #577, #599). - Detect the browser's interface language, with manual language selection and expanded translations across seven languages. Add audio-only input and subtitle generation, searchable API model selection, and background task pause/resume/stop controls. Controls take effect at processing checkpoints; an in-flight model or network call may need to finish first (#537, #576). - Reuse transcription from `.cache/asr` by source-file content and recognition settings. Successful segments survive later failures; complete cache hits skip recognition, including after output cleanup or reupload. Translation and TTS are not included in this cache (#608, incorporating the original work in #607; fixes #562). - Add optional `youtube.proxy` for yt-dlp downloads: omitted/null keeps native proxy discovery, an empty string forces a direct connection, and a URL selects an explicit proxy (#602).  🐛 Bug Fixes  - Request MP4 for yt-dlp merges and fix Windows Unicode console failures; keep launcher logs consistently UTF-8 (#578, #579, #602). - Reconcile extracted audio with source presentation timestamps, addressing cumulative subtitle drift caused by timestamp gaps or overlaps in both video and audio-only inputs (#601). - Preserve ElevenLabs word-level timestamps for downstream processing, and keep manually selected or automatically detected recognition language consistent with NLP and translation prompts (#576, #606; fixes #549). - Fix SiliconFlow Fish custom-voice creation, validate returned voice identifiers, and preserve useful API errors (#604; fixes #572). - Accept legitimate input videos with names starting with `output`, while still excluding generated output files and rejecting ambiguous inputs (#605, based on #597; fixes #596). - Fix pandas 3 / NumPy-related dubbing duration and timestamp-table failures, support legacy numeric timestamp literals safely, and run Edge TTS with the active Python interpreter (#599, #602). - Improve GPT-SoVITS startup checks by verifying its API schema, detecting exited child processes, and avoiding application-wide working-directory changes (#602). - Restore final dubbed-video audio to AAC 192 kbps. Measure gated loudness with FFmpeg, then apply a single gain with a true-peak limit so silence padding does not drive excessive amplification. Surface video assembly failures correctly. This processing applies to final dubbed-video assembly, not standalone `dub.mp3` (#610; related to #533). - Restore default raw audio preparation to 32 kHz / 128 kbps, add peak protection and silence handling to reference/vocal normalization, and restore SiliconFlow Fish merged reference audio to 44.1 kHz. Raw extraction settings are configurable through `audio.raw_sample_rate` and `audio.raw_bitrate`; old configs use the defaults when those keys are absent (#611; related to #533).  🔧 Improvements  - Create new uv environments with Python 3.13; existing Python 3.10–3.13 environments remain supported. Retain the matched Torch/torchaudio 2.8.0 and torchvision 0.23.0 family, update WhisperX to the 3.8.6+ compatible range and TorchCodec to 0.7, and keep Transformers below 5. Use bounded dependency ranges and optional PyPI Demucs 4.1 instead of a Git installation workaround (#599). - Reuse complete local, project, or global Whisper model caches before downloading missing files. Remove per-segment mirror probes; this does not guarantee every alignment/model component is already available offline (#577, #602). - Change the default LLM configuration to OpenRouter with `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash`. Other compatible endpoints and model names remain configurable. New default configurations disable Demucs vocal separation; enable it when needed (#592, #576). - Remove the retired 302.ai WhisperX cloud recognition backend. Local WhisperX and ElevenLabs recognition remain available, and separate 302.ai TTS integrations remain in the code (#609; fixes #583). - Align Docker, Colab, and local setup with the same installer. Docker builds the checked-out source with explicit cu128 wheels by default, with a cu126 image option; Colab runs the app in a separate Python 3.13 environment. Both automated setup calls use `--yes`. Add matching Torch build checks, Windows FFmpeg DLL discovery, a TorchCodec load probe, and corrected Linux Noto CJK font detection (#612).  ⚠️ Upgrade Notes  - **CUDA wheel selection:** on NVIDIA GPU systems, `auto` installation/reuse and health checks accept only matching cu126 or cu128 Torch/torchaudio/torchvision builds. cu129 is no longer accepted by this policy. Explicit choices are `--torch-backend cpu`, `cu126`, or `cu128`; they do not bypass the supported-build policy. For RTX 50 / Blackwell, use cu128 with a compatible driver, not cu126. Removing cu129 selection does not itself remove Blackwell support: PyTorch introduced Blackwell support in its CUDA 12.8 wheels ([upstream announcement](https://pytorch.org/blog/pytorch-2-7/)). A complete VideoLingo run on Blackwell was not validated for these changes. - **FFmpeg:** TorchCodec 0.7 requires FFmpeg 4–7 shared libraries. On Windows, follow the [FFmpeg 7 shared-build setup](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/pages/docs/start.en-US.md#ffmpeg-runtime). FFmpeg 8/9 alone is insufficient, and a clean `pip check` does not establish that TorchCodec can load. - **Retired ASR configuration:** change `whisper.runtime: cloud` to `local` or `elevenlabs` and configure the chosen backend. Old cloud configurations are reported explicitly; the app does not silently switch providers. - **Existing audio and caches:** extraction/timing fixes do not regenerate existing working files. Back up needed outputs and regenerate the affected audio and downstream results to apply them. For a fresh recognition result after the timing fix, also bypass the persistent cache with `whisper.cache: false` or clear the relevant cache. SiliconFlow Fish custom voices may need recreation. See the [audio regeneration notes](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/audio-extract-quality.md) and [transcription cache guide](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/transcription-cache.md). Higher extraction quality increases disk and memory use. The audio fixes address measured processing behavior; they do not claim to resolve every voice-quality or sentence-continuity report in #533. - **Validation scope:** the deployment work includes Windows and default Docker component checks, but the full Docker build preceded the final checker changes. Actual Colab execution, the cu126 image variant, and a complete GPU Docker pipeline were not validated. See the [deployment verification record](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/deployment-versions.md#verification-boundary).  ---  更新日志 - 版本 3.0.4  本次发布汇总 v3.0.1 以来的改动，包含源码中曾标为 3.0.2、3.0.3、但未单独发布 tag 的工作。主要包括安装与界面改进、转写结果持久缓存、依赖与部署更新，以及针对字幕时间轴和配音音频处理的修复。  🚀 新功能  - 支持通过 uv 创建项目独立或共享环境。分阶段安装器可重复运行；OneKeyStart 启动前检查环境、修复缺失或过期的依赖并记录日志。`--check-only` 仅检查，不修复或启动应用（#532、#537、#577、#599）。 - 根据浏览器语言选择界面语言，支持手动切换并补全七种语言的翻译。新增纯音频输入与字幕生成、API 模型搜索选择，以及后台任务暂停／继续／停止控制。控制在处理检查点生效，正在进行的模型或网络调用可能需要先完成（#537、#576）。 - 根据源文件内容和识别设置复用 `.cache/asr` 转写缓存。后续片段失败时保留已成功的片段；清理输出或重新上传相同文件后，完整命中可跳过识别。此缓存不包含翻译或 TTS 结果（由 #608 合入 #607 的原始工作；修复 #562）。 - 新增 yt-dlp 下载专用的可选 `youtube.proxy`：省略或设为 null 时保留原生代理发现，空字符串强制直连，URL 指定代理（#602）。  🐛 问题修复  - yt-dlp 合并时指定 MP4，修复 Windows Unicode 控制台报错，并统一启动器日志的 UTF-8 编码（#578、#579、#602）。 - 提取音频时按源媒体呈现时间戳对齐，修复时间戳间隙或重叠导致的字幕累计漂移，覆盖视频与纯音频输入（#601）。 - 保留 ElevenLabs 的词级时间戳供后续处理使用；使手动选择或自动检测的识别语言与 NLP、翻译提示词保持一致（#576、#606；修复 #549）。 - 修复 SiliconFlow Fish 自定义音色创建，校验返回的音色标识，并保留有效的 API 错误信息（#604；修复 #572）。 - 正确识别文件名以 `output` 开头的合法输入视频，同时继续排除生成产物并拒绝有歧义的多输入（#605，基于 #597；修复 #596）。 - 修复 pandas 3／NumPy 相关的配音时长与时间戳表格问题，安全读取旧版数值时间戳表达式，并使用当前 Python 解释器运行 Edge TTS（#599、#602）。 - 改进 GPT-SoVITS 启动检查：验证 API schema、识别已退出的子进程，并避免修改整个应用的工作目录（#602）。 - 将配音成片音频恢复为 AAC 192 kbps。通过 FFmpeg 测量门限响度后施加统一增益，并限制真峰值，避免静音填充导致过度放大；视频合成失败时正确报错。此处理仅用于配音视频合成，不影响单独的 `dub.mp3`（#610；关联 #533）。 - 将原始音频准备的默认参数恢复为 32 kHz／128 kbps，为参考音频／人声归一化增加峰值保护和静音处理，并将 SiliconFlow Fish 合并参考音频恢复为 44.1 kHz。可通过 `audio.raw_sample_rate`、`audio.raw_bitrate` 配置提取参数；旧配置缺键时使用默认值（#611；关联 #533）。  🔧 改进  - 新建 uv 环境使用 Python 3.13，已有 Python 3.10–3.13 环境仍受支持。保留配套的 Torch／torchaudio 2.8.0 和 torchvision 0.23.0，将 WhisperX 更新至兼容的 3.8.6+ 范围、TorchCodec 更新至 0.7，并保持 Transformers 低于 5。采用有上下界的依赖范围，以可选的 PyPI Demucs 4.1 替代原来的 Git 安装变通方案（#599）。 - 优先复用完整的本地、项目或全局 Whisper 模型缓存，再下载缺失文件；移除逐片段镜像探测。这不保证所有对齐模型等组件均已具备离线运行条件（#577、#602）。 - 默认 LLM 配置改为 OpenRouter 的 `deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash`，仍可配置其他兼容端点和模型。新配置默认关闭 Demucs 人声分离，可按需开启（#592、#576）。 - 移除已停用的 302.ai WhisperX 云识别后端；保留本地 WhisperX、ElevenLabs 识别，以及代码中独立的 302.ai TTS 集成（#609；修复 #583）。 - Docker、Colab 与本地使用同一安装器。Docker 从当前检出的源码构建，默认显式安装 cu128，另提供 cu126 镜像选项；Colab 在独立 Python 3.13 环境运行应用。两个自动安装入口均传入 `--yes`。新增 Torch 构建一致性检查、Windows FFmpeg DLL 发现与 TorchCodec 加载探测，并修正 Linux Noto CJK 字体检测（#612）。  ⚠️ 升级注意  - **CUDA 构建选择：** NVIDIA GPU 环境下，`auto` 安装／复用及健康检查仅接受构建一致的 cu126 或 cu128 Torch／torchaudio／torchvision，cu129 不再属于此策略允许的构建。显式选项为 `--torch-backend cpu`、`cu126` 或 `cu128`，不能用来绕过支持范围。RTX 50／Blackwell 应使用 cu128 和兼容驱动，不应选择 cu126。取消 cu129 自动选择本身不代表取消 Blackwell 支持：PyTorch 已在 CUDA 12.8 构建中引入 Blackwell 支持（[上游说明](https://pytorch.org/blog/pytorch-2-7/)）。这些改动未经过完整 VideoLingo Blackwell 流程验证。 - **FFmpeg：** TorchCodec 0.7 需要 FFmpeg 4–7 共享库。Windows 请按[FFmpeg 7 共享构建指南](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/pages/docs/start.zh-CN.md#ffmpeg-runtime)配置。只有 FFmpeg 8/9 不够；`pip check` 通过也不能证明 TorchCodec 可以加载。 - **旧云识别配置：** 将 `whisper.runtime: cloud` 改为 `local` 或 `elevenlabs`，并配置所选后端。应用会明确提示旧云配置问题，不会静默切换服务商。 - **已有音频与缓存：** 提取和时间轴修复不会自动重建已有工作文件。请先备份需要保留的输出，再重新生成相关音频及后续结果。若要在时间轴修复后重新识别，还需通过 `whisper.cache: false` 绕过持久缓存，或清理相关缓存；SiliconFlow Fish 自定义音色可能需要重新创建。参见[音频重建说明](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/audio-extract-quality.md)与[转写缓存指南](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/transcription-cache.md)。提高提取质量会增加磁盘和内存占用。这些音频修复针对可测的处理行为，不宣称解决 #533 中全部音色或句间连贯性问题。 - **验证范围：** 部署改动包含 Windows 和默认 Docker 的组件检查，但完整 Docker 构建早于最后的检查器修改。实际 Colab 运行、cu126 镜像变体及完整 GPU Docker 流程尚未验证，详见[部署验证记录](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/blob/main/docs/deployment-versions.md#verification-boundary)。  Full Changelog / 完整改动：[v3.0.1...v3.0.4](https://github.com/Huanshere/VideoLingo/compare/v3.0.1...v3.0.4)
```

### 2026-09-09T21:10:23Z [**Fission-AI/OpenSpec**](https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec)

```
## What's New in v1.13.0  Archive and the delta parser stop quietly changing or dropping what you wrote, and apply now tells you when a change has no specs.  ### New  - **Apply flags a change with no delta specs** - `openspec instructions apply` used to report a change as ready whenever its tasks existed, even with no spec deltas at all, which is the state `openspec validate` rejects. It now warns in both text and `--json`, and names both ways out: write the specs, or declare `skip_specs: true`.  ### Improved  - **Explore finds your existing specs** - Generated guidance never named `openspec list --specs`, so an agent asked to read the current specs enumerated in-flight changes instead and reported the step done against the wrong thing. Explore now lists the spec inventory beside the change list, and reads a capability with the store-aware command. - **Init and update name the workflows your profile left out** - A `/opsx:` command that was never installed used to read as a broken setup. Both commands now say which workflows are missing and how to add them. - **Propose reads project context before planning** - Context is loaded from the selected project or store root before any planning decision. In a directory with no OpenSpec root, propose stops without writing and offers to initialize rather than creating one silently.  ### Fixed  - **Archive no longer edits your fenced code** - A requirement documenting a sample with two or more blank lines in a row had that sample rewritten on every archive. Blank-line tidying is now fence-aware, which matters for YAML, Python, and expected-output fixtures. - **Removals and renames written with `*` or `+` now take effect** - CommonMark allows all three bullet markers, but only `-` was read. A removal or rename written the other way matched nothing: validate passed, archive exited 0 reporting success, and the requirement stayed exactly as it was. - **Every delta section applies, not just one copy** - A file with two `## ADDED Requirements` sections silently kept one of them, so archive applied less than was written and moved the change to the archive anyway. You do not have to repeat a header deliberately to hit this: a delta documenting OpenSpec's own syntax inside a fenced example produces the duplicate on its own. - **Capabilities whose bullets wrap can be retired again** - `retire_capabilities` refused any spec whose scenario bullets ran onto a second line, which is most of them in a repo that wraps prose at a column limit. Specs bulleted with `+` are covered too. - **Update repairs damaged command files** - `openspec update` decided a tool was current from its skill files alone, so a hand-edited or truncated command file left it printing "All 1 tool(s) up to date" and repairing nothing. It now compares command-file content as well.  ## New Contributors  * @dwin-gharibi made their first contribution in https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/pull/1800  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.12.0...v1.13.0 
```

```
# GPUI Kit v0.6.1  GPUI Kit v0.6.1 brings multi-cursor editing, automatic bracket pairing, smart indentation, and headless UI testing. This release also improves Markdown rendering, text selection, accessibility, and everyday component behavior.  ## Editor and Text Input  - **Multi-cursor editing** with Alt-click to add cursors and Shift-Alt-drag for column selection. Column selections now preserve their width across short lines. ([#2837](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2837), [#2987](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2987)) - **Automatic bracket pairing and smart indentation** on Enter, with configurable language rules and independent `auto_close` and `smart_indent` preferences. ([#3018](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3018)) - Improved search behavior: reopening search restores focus, preserves the active match, and reveals matches after manual scrolling. ([#2932](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2932), [#2953](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2953), [#2955](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2955)) - Fixed cursor movement across CRLF boundaries and pending syntax highlighting when switching languages. ([#2986](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2986), [#2994](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2994)) - Fixed selection drag-autoscroll in virtualized lists and ensured scrolling stops when the selection gesture ends. ([#2948](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2948), [#2941](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2941)) - Reduced unnecessary updates when scroll and paint state remain unchanged. ([#2988](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2988))  ## Headless UI Testing  Applications can now test interactions and layout through `gpui_kit::test`, enabled by the `test-support` feature. The testing API supports scoped queries, native input events, state snapshots, and waiting for deferred updates.  This release also expands integration coverage across core controls, menus, dialogs, tables, and dock layouts, with separate Metal rendering checks on macOS. ([#3005](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3005), [#3019](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3019))  ## Markdown and Text Rendering  - Added opt-in **YAML frontmatter rendering** through `MarkdownExtensions::frontmatter()` and `FrontmatterPlugin`. Supported mappings render as structured metadata; unsupported content falls back to a YAML code block. ([#2850](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2850)) - Corrected hard and soft line-break rendering. ([#2945](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2945), [#2947](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2947)) - Improved inline code styling with rounded backgrounds and a monospace font. ([#2949](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2949)) - Fixed stale measurements when replacing a document with another containing the same number of blocks. ([#2946](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2946))  ## Components and Accessibility  - **Button:** added preferred tooltip placement and restored the selected background for ghost buttons. ([#2971](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2971), [#2938](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2938)) - **Select:** exposed committed values, improved accessible activation, and made `on_dismiss` run consistently regardless of how the popup closes. ([#2973](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2973), [#2984](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2984)) - **Sidebar:** added `Styled` support and `label_style` to `SidebarMenuItem`. ([#2957](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2957)) - **Icons:** added SVG byte support in component icon slots and shared Lucide icon names while preserving default icons. ([#2980](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2980), [#3020](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3020)) - Fixed panel resizing flicker and list measurement when the configured measurement row is absent. ([#2907](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2907), [#2975](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2975)) - Improved accessibility for dialog close buttons and calendar items. ([#2969](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2969), [#2996](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2996)) - Added fallback to an installed monospace font when the default is unavailable. ([#3009](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/3009))  ## Runtime and Platform Improvements  - Refined FPS reporting using frame cost and the display's refresh rate, with sustained frames enabled by default. ([#2944](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2944), [#2954](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2954), [#2956](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2956)) - Restricted Windows native-menu `uxtheme` loading to System32. ([#2974](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2974)) - Updated Tree-sitter and removed the previous workaround. ([#2990](https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2990))  ## Documentation  The documentation website now uses Astro, with improved App Stories browsing and preserved sidebar scroll position. Component API documentation is separated from shared Kit guides, with expanded coverage of fonts, editor search, UI testing, and AI-assisted development.  Thank you to everyone who contributed code, documentation, testing, and feedback!  ## What's Changed * website: Bundle IBM Plex Sans so `.SystemUIFont` resolves on the web by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2933 * gpui-pre: Audit licenses before publishing and state GPUI's origin by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2936 * chore: Let the doctests written for gpui-kit users compile by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2937 * website: Add a release notes page and keep page titles off the toolbar by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2935 * button: Select a ghost button with `secondary_active` again by @madcodelife in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2938 * shell: Improve application compatibility by @huacnlee in https://github.com/longbridge/gpui-kit/pull/2939 * chroe: Drop the unused reqwest_client dependency by @huacnlee in # Changelog  ## ✨ 新增功能 - 新增部署提供商：腾讯云 DDoS 高防 - 新增部署提供商：橙域网络网迹 CDN - 部署证书到 SSH 远程主机时支持以 REPL 模式执行前、后置命令 - 部署证书到火山引擎 CLB 传统型负载均衡时支持指定扩展域名 - 部署证书到 LeCDN 时支持 LeCDN v4 - LeCDN 授权凭据中支持选择 API 认证方式为 API Key  ## 🐛 修复 Bug - 修复 #1452：新网 DNS API 接口返回 404 错误 - 修复 #1453：部署证书到 Kubernetes 时当 Secret 不存在时返回 405 错误 - 修复因 DCloud 登录接口变更导致无法部署证书到 uniCloud 的问题  ## 🛠 优化 & 其他改动 - 升级 gomod 依赖项 - 升级 npm 依赖项  ---  Comparing changes: https://github.com/certimate-go/certimate/compare/v0.4.32...v0.4.33, by @bangbang93, @fudiwei, @gdyr, @usual2970, @xinyihl
```

### 2026-09-11T16:04:18Z [**devld/go-drive**](https://github.com/devld/go-drive)

```
## Highlights  * Script Drives now run on Goja with modern JavaScript syntax, safer host bindings, immutable Go-backed views, clearer errors, and context-aware execution. * Admin Jobs now show live progress for running operations and provide paginated execution history with a clearer task and log view. * Script Drives gain explicit progress reporting, Drive-local intervals, forced OAuth token refresh, URL helpers, and JSON response parsing. * File-system paths now reject Windows-style backslash traversal sequences.  ## Breaking Changes  * **Script Drives.** Scripts must be migrated from the old Otto/ES5 API to the Goja API. Host fields and methods now use lowerCamelCase (`entry.path`, `resp.status`, `resp.json()`, `oauthLoad`, etc.); old PascalCase names and Otto context parameters are not compatible. * **Script Drive operations.** Lifecycle and operation signatures changed. In particular, `save`, `copy`, `move`, and `delete` receive explicit progress capabilities. Uploading scripts should wrap the consumed reader with `reader.withProgress(progress)` as documented. * **Logging.** Replace `GO_DRIVE_DEBUG=1` with `GO_DRIVE_LOGGING_LEVEL=debug`. The same setting can be configured as `logging.level` in `config.yml`.  ## Migration  1. Back up `data-dir` and `config.yml`, then upgrade as usual. 2. Open **Admin → Other Drives**, refresh/pull the Script Drive repository, and update every installed Script Drive. 3. Migrate custom scripts to the current template and declarations, including lowerCamel names, the new lifecycle signatures, and explicit progress handling. 4. Confirm sign-in, Drive reload, uploads, scheduled work, and jobs after upgrading.  ## New Features and Improvements  * Drive scripts can schedule recurring background work and force an OAuth token refresh when needed. * `urlUtils` and `HttpResponse.json()` simplify URL and JSON handling in scripts. * The script editor provides better JavaScript suggestions, including suggestions inside string literals. * Script and native function names are clearer in logs and stack traces; Drive handles also have more useful log and JSON representations. * Action and trigger definitions retain a stable registration order.  ## Bug Fixes  * Windows-style path traversal through file-system Drives is blocked. * Upload progress is reported consistently for script-backed HTTP operations.  **Full Changelog**: https://github.com/devld/go-drive/compare/v0.16.1...v0.17.0 
```

### 2026-09-11T03:42:25Z [**cokice/japanese-analyzer**](https://github.com/cokice/japanese-analyzer)

```
## 更新亮点  - **液态玻璃界面**：重新打磨主页、聊天、设置和词语详情，统一浅色与深色视觉；输入工具采用玻璃质感，解析按钮位置更稳定，点击 Logo 可刷新返回首页。 - **词典式释义**：精简词语解释，突出读音、词性、释义、本句用法与例句，减少冗长的生成式表达。 - **阅读与解析体验**：优化长文章分段解析、流式结果展示及滚动区域，修复译文遮挡，支持终止解析，并完善首次访问示例引导。 - **本地罗马音**：根据假名在本地生成罗马音，减少模型需要输出的重复字段；中文提示与日文正文分别使用对应字体。 - **模型与识图**：统一 DeepSeek 文本与识图模型为 `deepseek-flash`，Gemini 文本模型使用 latest 别名。 - **隐私统计**：新增解析成功、失败、取消，以及聊天发送、回复成功和失败事件，记录首批结果耗时与总耗时；不记录原文、聊天内容、密钥或错误正文。 - **文档**：更新中文 README、日文 README 和界面截图。  ## 相关合并  - #60 在线文档入口 - #61 首次访问示例引导 - #62 DeepSeek 深度思考与长文本解析体验 - #65 DeepSeek 文本与识图模型统一 - #66 Gemini latest 模型别名 - #67 中日文 README 更新 - #68 液态玻璃界面与词语详情优化 - #69 解析与聊天请求结果统计 - #70 输入控件玻璃样式与 Logo 返回首页  ## 验证  - 发布内容与主分支 `4987675` 一致，`npm test` 全部通过。 - 本轮界面调整已验证浅色、深色及窄屏布局、按钮稳定性、语音菜单和 Logo 返回首页行为。  **完整变更**：[v0.2.3 → v0.3.0](https://github.com/cokice/japanese-analyzer/compare/v0.2.3...v0.3.0) 
```

### 2026-09-14T11:27:11Z [**AChep/keyguard-app**](https://github.com/AChep/keyguard-app)

```
### New *   Linux AppImage builds now support zsync delta updates. *   Added GPG public certificate validation.  ### Agents *   Improves SSH and GPG agent reliability on Android. *   GPG key details now respect configured policies. *   SSH and GPG keys are revalidated after an agent request is approved.  ### Platforms *   Improves reliability when running multiple app instances on Windows. *   Improves system tray icon reliability on desktop platforms.  ### Fixes *   Syncing local accounts no longer requires an active internet connection. *   The 'Mark all as read' action in Watchtower now applies only to the currently filtered items. *   Moving an item to the root of a KDBX database is now saved correctly. *   Creating a Bitwarden Send now requires selecting a destination account. *   Changing backup settings no longer resets the retention policy. *   Ensures the latest master password is used for authentication. *   Improves WebDAV sync reliability. *   The vault item details screen now updates immediately when the Markdown notes preference is changed. *   Fixes progress display for cancelled or failed vault exports. *   Invalid regular expressions are now rejected in input fields. *   The key generator shows an OpenPGP-specific tip for RSA keys. *   The color picker selects the nearest available palette color. 
```

### 2026-09-15T00:29:37Z [**gildas-lormeau/SingleFile**](https://github.com/gildas-lormeau/SingleFile)

```
### Changes  - A save in progress can be cancelled, from a Cancel button in the saving mask or with the Escape key, registered at `document_start` so it precedes any page listener (#1994) - New option bounding the zoom out applied to the page while deferred content is loaded - The deferred images options are renamed to deferred content, and the section now comes before the network section in the options page - New option to save the canonical link of the page, which can be turned off - The MAFF metadata option is renamed and its two defects are fixed: it recovers the original URL and date of the archived page - The interface shown while saving follows `prefers-color-scheme: dark` - The icon of the infobar added to saved pages shows a ripple animation (#1504) - Multi-page archives open on their table of contents and honor the root directory option, and one re-saved from the editor keeps its pages under the same root - `single-file-core` updated to 1.6.1, which brings zip.js 2.15.0  ### Fixes  - Saved pages can no longer submit forms or set a base URI, and `javascript:` URIs are sanitized in every attribute and namespace - The saving mask is removed when a capture fails, instead of staying on screen with the Escape shortcut still armed - The Escape key that cancels an area selection is swallowed on the window in the capture phase and no longer reaches the page - The content script UI is isolated from the page - The page document is exempt from the resource size cap - The bytes of a resource are trusted over its declared content type - Response headers are relayed in full and looked up case-insensitively - Page content is sent as a Blob rather than an array where the messaging preserves it - `FileReader` is feature-detected in the extension UI, so importing settings, choosing a custom archive image and saving to GitHub work in Safari with Lockdown Mode enabled - `build-extension.sh` requires `zip`, and `jq` on MV3, up front instead of attempting `sudo apt install` 
```

```
## Release Notes  Released on 2026-09-10.  ### Preview features  - \[`ruff`\] Add rule for default values on method receivers (`RUF077`) ([#26700](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/26700)) - \[`ruff`\] Recognize `re.prefixmatch` (`RUF039`, `RUF055`) ([#28311](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28311))  ### Bug fixes  - Alternate nested quotes inside format spec interpolations ([#28259](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28259)) - \[`flake8-implicit-str-concat`\] Mark fix unsafe when it creates a docstring (`ISC003`) ([#27981](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/27981)) - \[`flake8-tidy-imports`\] Skip fixes for multi-member imports (`TID254`) ([#26584](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/26584)) - \[`pylint`\] Gate `ImportCycleError` on Python 3.15 (`PLW0133`) ([#28310](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28310))  ### Rule changes  - Correct `D211` and `D203` rule conflict diagnostic ([#28444](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28444)) - Recognize `slice` and `frozendict` generics ([#28477](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28477)) - Stop defining `__cached__` for Python 3.15 ([#28476](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28476)) - \[`pyupgrade`\] Stop recommending removed `typing.no_type_check_decorator` (`UP035`) ([#28475](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28475))  ### Performance  - Reuse parser name lookups when interning ([#28399](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28399)) - Speed up inherited configuration resolution ([#28299](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28299))  ### Documentation  - Fix `line-length` path in `--config` example ([#28392](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28392)) - Remove the "Who’s Using Ruff?" list ([#28455](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28455))  ### Other changes  - Embed archive checksums in the shell installer ([#28281](https://github.com/astral-sh/ruff/pull/28281))  ### Contributors  - [@The-Compiler](https://github.com/The-Compiler) - [@mdiniz97](https://github.com/mdiniz97) -
```
